"use client";

import React from "react";
import { Package, UserCheck, Users, ClipboardCheck, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import DetailSection from "./DetailSection";
import DetailField from "./DetailField";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/utils/dateFormat";

interface PengambilanDetailContentProps {
  claim: any;
  onToast?: (message: string, type: "success" | "error") => void;
}

export default function PengambilanDetailContent({ claim, onToast }: PengambilanDetailContentProps) {
  if (!claim) return null;

  const foundItem = claim.foundItem || {};

  // Identitas Pengambil / Penerima
  const claimantId =
    claim.claimantIdCard && claim.claimantIdCard !== "-"
      ? claim.claimantIdCard
      : claim.wargaId || claim.warga?.id || "â€”";
  const claimantName = claim.warga?.nama || claim.claimantName || "â€”";
  const claimantContact = claim.claimantContact && claim.claimantContact !== "-" ? claim.claimantContact : claim.warga?.nomorTelepon || "â€”";
  const claimantPeran = claim.warga?.peran ? `${claim.warga.peran}${claim.warga.keteranganPeran ? ` (${claim.warga.keteranganPeran})` : ""}` : null;

  // Identitas Penemu (HARUS JELAS TERPISAH)
  const finderId =
    foundItem.finderIdCard && foundItem.finderIdCard !== "-"
      ? foundItem.finderIdCard
      : foundItem.wargaId || foundItem.warga?.id || "â€”";
  const finderName = foundItem.warga?.nama || foundItem.finderName || "â€”";
  const finderContact = foundItem.finderContact && foundItem.finderContact !== "-" ? foundItem.finderContact : foundItem.warga?.nomorTelepon || "â€”";
  const finderPeran = foundItem.warga?.peran ? `${foundItem.warga.peran}${foundItem.warga.keteranganPeran ? ` (${foundItem.warga.keteranganPeran})` : ""}` : null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* BAGIAN 1: INFORMASI BARANG */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<Package className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Informasi Barang yang Diambil"
        badge={
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-blue-100 text-blue-800">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
            Diambil
          </span>
        }
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Kode Barang"
            value={foundItem.businessCode}
            mono
            copyable
            copyValue={foundItem.businessCode}
            onToast={onToast}
            className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-1"
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Jenis / Nama Barang"
            value={foundItem.jenis?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Merek"
            value={foundItem.merek?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Warna"
            value={foundItem.warna?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Lokasi Penemuan"
            value={foundItem.lokasi?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Waktu Ditemukan"
            value={formatDateTime(foundItem.createdAt)}
          />
          {foundItem.additionalDesc && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3 pt-1">
              <DetailField
                label="Keterangan Barang"
                value={foundItem.additionalDesc}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </DetailSection>

      {/* BAGIAN 2: IDENTITAS PENGAMBIL / PENERIMA */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<UserCheck className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Identitas Pengambil"
        badge={
          <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800">
            Penerima Barang
          </span>
        }
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Nama Lengkap Pengambil"
            value={claimantName}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="ID (NIS / NIP)"
            value={claimantId}
            mono
            copyable={claimantId !== "â€”"}
            copyValue={claimantId}
            onToast={onToast}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Kontak"
            value={claimantContact}
          />
          {claimantPeran && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3">
              <DetailField
                label="Status Pengambil"
                value={claimantPeran}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </DetailSection>

      {/* BAGIAN 3: IDENTITAS PENEMU*/}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<Users className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Identitas Penemu"
        badge={
          <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
            Penemu
          </span>
        }
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Nama Penemu"
            value={finderName}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="ID Penemu"
            value={finderId}
            mono
            copyable={finderId !== "â€”"}
            copyValue={finderId}
            onToast={onToast}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Kontak Penemu"
            value={finderContact}
          />
          {finderPeran && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3">
              <DetailField
                label="Status Penemu"
                value={finderPeran}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </DetailSection>

      {/* BAGIAN 4: INFORMASI SERAH TERIMA */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<ClipboardCheck className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Informasi Serah Terima & Transaksi"
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Waktu Serah Terima"
            value={formatDateTime(claim.claimedAt)}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Lokasi Serah Terima"
            value="LACAK"
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Status Transaksi"
            value="Selesai"
          />
          <DetailField
            label="ID Transaksi"
            value={claim.id}
            mono
            copyable
            copyValue={claim.id}
            onToast={onToast}
          />
        </div>

FILE: src\components\riwayat\RiwayatActionDropdown.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { MoreHorizontal, Trash2, Eye, Copy, Check } from "lucide-react";

interface RiwayatActionDropdownProps {
  onDelete: () => void;
  onDetail: () => void;
  itemCode?: string | null;
  copyDisabledReason?: string;
  onToast?: (message: string, type: "success" | "error") => void;
}

export default function RiwayatActionDropdown({
  onDelete,
  onDetail,
  itemCode,
  copyDisabledReason = "Kode barang belum tersedia",
  onToast,
}: RiwayatActionDropdownProps) {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isCopied, setIsCopied] = useState(false);
  const [menuPosition, setMenuPosition] = useState<{ top: number; left: number } | null>(null);
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const updatePosition = useCallback(() => {
    if (!buttonRef.current) return;
    const rect = buttonRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const menuWidth = 190;
    const menuHeight = 135;

    // Hitung posisi horizontal (prioritaskan align right dengan button)
    let left = rect.right - menuWidth;
    if (left < 10) left = 10;
    if (left + menuWidth > window.innerWidth - 10) {
      left = window.innerWidth - menuWidth - 10;
    }

    // Hitung posisi vertikal (bawah jika cukup, atau ke atas jika mepet bawah layar)
    let top = rect.bottom + 6;
    if (top + menuHeight > window.innerHeight - 10) {
      top = rect.top - menuHeight - 6;
    }

    setMenuPosition({ top, left });
  }, []);

  const handleToggle = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!isOpen) {
      updatePosition();
      setIsOpen(true);
    } else {
      setIsOpen(false);
    }
  };

  // Close on outside click, window resize or scroll
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleOutsideClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (
        menuRef.current &&
        !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node) &&
        buttonRef.current &&
        !buttonRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)
      ) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleScrollOrResize = () => {
      setIsOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScrollOrResize, true);
    window.addEventListener("resize", handleScrollOrResize);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScrollOrResize, true);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", handleScrollOrResize);
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleCopy = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!itemCode) return;

    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(itemCode);
      } else {
        // Fallback untuk environment non-HTTPS atau testing
        const textArea = document.createElement("textarea");
        textArea.value = itemCode;
        textArea.style.position = "fixed";
        textArea.style.opacity = "0";
        document.body.appendChild(textArea);
        textArea.select();
        document.execCommand("copy");
        document.body.removeChild(textArea);
      }
      setIsCopied(true);
      onToast?.(`Kode barang "${itemCode}" berhasil disalin ke clipboard!`, "success");
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIsCopied(false);
        setIsOpen(false);
      }, 300);
    } catch {
      onToast?.("Gagal menyalin kode barang.", "error");
      setIsOpen(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsOpen(false);
    onDelete();
  };

  const handleDetail = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsOpen(false);
    onDetail();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button
        ref={buttonRef}
        type="button"
        onClick={handleToggle}
        aria-label="Menu Aksi"
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition-all cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center justify-center ${
          isOpen
            ? "bg-[#0d3b2e] text-white border-[#0d3b2e] shadow-xs"
            : "bg-white text-gray-600 border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900"
        }`}
      >
        <MoreHorizontal className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>

      {isOpen &&
        menuPosition &&
        typeof document !== "undefined" &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            ref={menuRef}
            role="menu"
            aria-orientation="vertical"
            style={{
              position: "fixed",
              top: `${menuPosition.top}px`,
              left: `${menuPosition.left}px`,
              zIndex: 9999,
            }}
            className="w-48 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 py-1.5 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150 focus:outline-hidden"
          >
            {/* 1. HAPUS */}
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={handleDelete}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50/80 transition-colors text-left cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500 shrink-0" />
              <span>Hapus</span>
            </button>

            {/* 2. RINCIAN */}
            <button
              type="button"
              role="menuitem"
              onClick={handleDetail}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 hover:bg-emerald-50/80 hover:text-[#0d3b2e] transition-colors text-left cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500 shrink-0" />
              <span>Rincian</span>
            </button>

            <div className="my-1 border-t border-gray-100" />

            {/* 3. SALIN KODE BARANG */}
            {itemCode ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={handleCopy}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-gray-900 transition-colors text-left cursor-pointer"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 truncate">
                  {isCopied ? (
                    <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <span className="truncate">Salin Kode Barang</span>
                </div>
              </button>
            ) : (
              <div
                role="menuitem"
                aria-disabled="true"
                title={copyDisabledReason}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed bg-gray-50/60"
              >
                <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-300 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">Salin Kode (N/A)</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>,
          document.body
        )}
    </>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src\components\riwayat\RiwayatDetailModal.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { X } from "lucide-react";

interface RiwayatDetailModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  badge?: React.ReactNode;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export default function RiwayatDetailModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  subtitle,
  badge,
  children,
}: RiwayatDetailModalProps) {
  const closeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        closeBtnRef.current?.focus();
      }, 50);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  // Lock body scroll when modal is open
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    }
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="presentation"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-6 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) {
          onClose();
        }
      }}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="detail-modal-title"
        className="w-full max-w-2xl sm:max-w-3xl bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-100 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200"
      >
        {/* Sticky Header */}
        <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-100 px-6 py-5 flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <h3 id="detail-modal-title" className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#0d3b2e] tracking-tight">
                {title}
              </h3>
              {badge && <div>{badge}</div>}
            </div>
            {subtitle && (
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium">{subtitle}</p>
            )}
          </div>
          <button
            ref={closeBtnRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 rounded-xl text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Tutup popup rincian"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Content */}
        <div className="p-6 space-y-6 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain flex-grow">
          {children}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="bg-gray-50/80 border-t border-gray-100 px-6 py-3.5 flex justify-end shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            Tutup
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import React from "react";
import { Package, UserCheck, ShieldCheck, CheckCircle2, Clock, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import DetailSection from "./DetailSection";
import DetailField from "./DetailField";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/lib/utils/dateFormat";

interface TemuanDetailContentProps {
  item: any;
  onToast?: (message: string, type: "success" | "error") => void;
}

export default function TemuanDetailContent({ item, onToast }: TemuanDetailContentProps) {
  if (!item) return null;

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "FOUND":
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
            Tersedia
          </span>
        );
      case "CLAIMED":
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-blue-100 text-blue-800">
            <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" />
            Diambil
          </span>
        );
      case "EXPIRED":
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
            Kedaluwarsa
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
            {status}
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  const finderId =
    item.finderIdCard && item.finderIdCard !== "-"
      ? item.finderIdCard
      : item.wargaId || item.warga?.id || "â€”";

  const finderName = item.warga?.nama || item.finderName || "â€”";
  const finderContact = item.finderContact && item.finderContact !== "-" ? item.finderContact : item.warga?.nomorTelepon || "â€”";
  const peranWarga = item.warga?.peran ? `${item.warga.peran}${item.warga.keteranganPeran ? ` (${item.warga.keteranganPeran})` : ""}` : null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {/* BAGIAN 1: INFORMASI BARANG */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<Package className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Informasi Barang"
        badge={getStatusBadge(item.status)}
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Kode Barang"
            value={item.businessCode}
            mono
            copyable
            copyValue={item.businessCode}
            onToast={onToast}
            className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-1"
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Jenis / Nama Barang"
            value={item.jenis?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Merek"
            value={item.merek?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Warna"
            value={item.warna?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Lokasi Penemuan"
            value={item.lokasi?.name}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Waktu Ditemukan / Terdaftar"
            value={formatDateTime(item.createdAt)}
          />
          <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3 pt-1">
            <DetailField
              label="Keterangan / Ciri Khusus"
              value={item.additionalDesc || "Tidak ada keterangan tambahan"}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </DetailSection>

      {/* BAGIAN 2: IDENTITAS PENEMU / PENYERAH */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<UserCheck className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Identitas Penemu / Penyerah"
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Nama Lengkap Penemu"
            value={finderName}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="ID Personel (NIS / NIP)"
            value={finderId}
            mono
            copyable={finderId !== "â€”"}
            copyValue={finderId}
            onToast={onToast}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Kontak / No. Telepon"
            value={finderContact}
          />
          {peranWarga && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 md:col-span-3">
              <DetailField
                label="Peran Personel Terdaftar"
                value={peranWarga}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </DetailSection>

      {/* BAGIAN 3: INFORMASI ADMINISTRATIF & SERAH TERIMA */}
      <DetailSection
        icon={<ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4" />}
        title="Informasi Administratif"
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <DetailField
            label="Waktu Pencatatan Sistem"
            value={formatDateTime(item.createdAt)}
          />
          <DetailField
            label="Terakhir Diperbarui"
            value={formatDateTime(item.updatedAt)}
          />

          {item.status === "CLAIMED" && item.claim && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 p-3.5 bg-blue-50/70 rounded-xl border border-blue-100 space-y-2 mt-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-900 font-bold text-xs">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600" />
                Catatan Serah Terima / Pengambilan
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-gray-500">Penerima:</span>{" "}
                  <strong className="text-gray-900 font-semibold">{item.claim.claimantName}</strong> (ID: {item.claim.claimantIdCard})
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-gray-500">Waktu Diambil:</span>{" "}
                  <span className="text-gray-900">{formatDateTime(item.claim.claimedAt)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {item.status === "FOUND" && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 text-xs text-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-100 p-3 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
              <span>Barang tersimpan di pos piket/penyimpanan dan siap diambil oleh pemilik yang sah.</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {item.status === "EXPIRED" && (
            <div className="sm:col-span-2 text-xs text-amber-800 bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-100 p-3 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
              <span>Masa simpan barang telah kedaluwarsa melewati batas hari operasional.</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </DetailSection>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\ChromeVisibility.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useState } from "react";

const ChromeVisibilityContext = createContext<{
  isNotFound: boolean;
  setIsNotFound: (isNotFound: boolean) => void;
} | null>(null);

export function ChromeVisibilityProvider({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const [isNotFound, setIsNotFound] = useState(false);

  return (
    <ChromeVisibilityContext.Provider value={{ isNotFound, setIsNotFound }}>
      {children}
    </ChromeVisibilityContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useChromeVisibility() {
  const context = useContext(ChromeVisibilityContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error(
      "useChromeVisibility must be used within ChromeVisibilityProvider."
    );
  }

  return context;
}

"use client";

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Trash2, X, Loader2, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

interface ConfirmAdminModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onConfirm: () => Promise<void> | void;
  title?: string;
  targetName?: string;
}

const REQUIRED_TEXT = "ini perintah admin!";

export default function ConfirmAdminModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  title = "Konfirmasi Hapus Semua Data",
  targetName = "seluruh data",
}: ConfirmAdminModalProps) {
  const [inputText, setInputText] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [prevIsOpen, setPrevIsOpen] = useState(isOpen);
  if (isOpen !== prevIsOpen) {
    setPrevIsOpen(isOpen);
    if (isOpen) {
      setInputText("");
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      // Auto focus after open
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        inputRef.current?.focus();
      }, 100);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && isOpen && !isLoading) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, isLoading, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isMatch = inputText.trim() === REQUIRED_TEXT;

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isMatch || isLoading) return;

    try {
      setIsLoading(true);
      await onConfirm();
      onClose();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Gagal melakukan penghapusan:", error);
      alert("Terjadi kesalahan saat menghapus data. Silakan coba lagi.");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150"
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget && !isLoading) {
          onClose();
        }
      }}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="confirm-modal-title"
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-red-100 overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-red-50/70 border-b border-red-100 p-5 flex items-start justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-red-100 text-red-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-xs">
              <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 id="confirm-modal-title" className="text-base font-bold text-gray-900 leading-snug">
                {title}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium mt-0.5">
                Tindakan Berbahaya & Permanen
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={isLoading}
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 p-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/80 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Tutup"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-5 space-y-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
            <p>
              Anda akan menghapus <strong className="text-gray-900">{targetName}</strong> secara permanen.
              Data yang telah dihapus tidak dapat dipulihkan kembali.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label htmlFor="confirmAdminInput" className="block text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wider">
              Konfirmasi Teks
            </label>
            <input
              id="confirmAdminInput"
              ref={inputRef}
              type="text"
              disabled={isLoading}
              value={inputText}
              onChange={(e) => setInputText(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={`Ketik "${REQUIRED_TEXT}"`}
              className={`w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-mono rounded-xl border transition-all outline-none ${
                isMatch
                  ? "border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50/30 text-emerald-900 ring-2 ring-emerald-500/20"
                  : inputText.length > 0
                  ? "border-red-300 bg-red-50/20 text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-400/20"
                  : "border-gray-300 bg-white text-gray-900 focus:border-red-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-400/20"
              }`}
            />
            
            {/* Status Feedback */}
            <div className="min-h-[18px]">
              {isMatch ? (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-1">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Konfirmasi sesuai. Siap dihapus.
                </span>
              ) : inputText.length > 0 ? (
                <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-red-500">
                  Teks konfirmasi belum sesuai.
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={isLoading}
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 cursor-pointer"
            >
              Batal
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={!isMatch || isLoading}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:bg-gray-200 disabled:text-gray-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
            >
              {isLoading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Menghapus...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Hapus Semua Sekarang</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";

export default function Navbar() {

    pathname === "/" ||

    isPetugasEmail(email.emailAddress)

    return (

              >
                <span className="w-4 h-0.5 bg-current rounded-full" />
                <span className="w-4 h-0.5 bg-current rounded-full" />
                <span className="w-4 h-0.5 bg-current rounded-full" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile Dropdown Menu */}
          {mobileMenuOpen && (
            <div className="md:hidden mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-white/15 flex flex-col gap-2 animate-fadeIn pb-2">
              <Link
                href="/tentang-kami"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className={`text-xs font-semibold py-2 px-3 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                  pathname === "/tentang-kami"
                    ? "bg-white/15 text-[#3dbd84]"
                    : "text-white/80 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                Tentang Kami
              </Link>

              <Link
                href="/beri-saran"
                onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
                className={`text-xs font-semibold py-2 px-3 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                  pathname === "/beri-saran"
                    ? "bg-white/15 text-[#3dbd84]"
                    : "text-white/80 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                Beri Saran
              </Link>

              {isLoaded && isSignedIn && isPetugas && (

                  href="/dashboard"
                  onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}

                >
                  Dashboard Petugas

              )}
            </div>

        </div>
      </header>
    );
  }

  return (
    <header className="bg-green-dark sticky top-0 z-[1030] shadow-md">
      
      {/* BARIS 1: Logo, SearchBar, Notifikasi & User Profile */}
      <div className="container mx-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-3">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] md:grid-cols-[120px_1fr_auto] gap-4 md:gap-10 items-center">

            LACAK
          </Link>

          <div className="w-full">

      <div className="border-t border-white/10 bg-green-dark relative z-[1020]">
        <NavTabs />
      </div>

    </header>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, useRef } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { LayoutDashboard, Users, History, ChevronDown, PackagePlus, FileSpreadsheet, PackageCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useClickOutside } from "@/hooks/useClickOutside";
import { useKeyboardShortcut } from "@/hooks/useKeyboardShortcut";

export default function NavTabs() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const isActive = (path: string) => pathname === path;
  const isRiwayatActive = pathname.startsWith("/riwayat");

  // Close on Click Outside
  useClickOutside(dropdownRef, () => {
    setIsDropdownOpen(false);
  });

  // Close on Escape
  useKeyboardShortcut("Escape", () => {
    setIsDropdownOpen(false);
  });

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto px-4 md:px-5">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-[120px_1fr_auto] gap-4 md:gap-10 items-center w-full">
        
        <div className="col-span-1 md:col-start-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 md:gap-2.5 overflow-visible py-2">
          
          {/* 1. Beranda */}
          <Link
            href="/dashboard"
            className={`whitespace-nowrap text-[0.875rem] md:text-[0.9rem] px-3.5 md:px-4 py-2.5 no-underline flex items-center gap-2 select-none ${
              isActive("/dashboard")
                ? "nav-tab-aurora-active"
                : "nav-tab-aurora-inactive"
            }`}
          >
            <LayoutDashboard className={`h-4 w-4 ${isActive("/dashboard") ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-white/70"}`} />
            <span>Beranda</span>
          </Link>

          {/* 2. Data Warga */}
          <Link
            href="/data-warga"
            className={`whitespace-nowrap text-[0.875rem] md:text-[0.9rem] px-3.5 md:px-4 py-2.5 no-underline flex items-center gap-2 select-none ${
              isActive("/data-warga")
                ? "nav-tab-aurora-active"
                : "nav-tab-aurora-inactive"
            }`}
          >
            <Users className={`h-4 w-4 ${isActive("/data-warga") ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-white/70"}`} />
            <span>Data Warga</span>
          </Link>

          {/* 3. Dropdown Riwayat */}
          <div className="relative flex-shrink-0" ref={dropdownRef}>
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(!isDropdownOpen)}
              aria-expanded={isDropdownOpen}
              aria-haspopup="true"
              className={`whitespace-nowrap text-[0.875rem] md:text-[0.9rem] px-3.5 md:px-4 py-2.5 cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 select-none ${
                isRiwayatActive
                  ? "nav-tab-aurora-active"
                  : "nav-tab-aurora-inactive"
              }`}
            >
              <History className={`h-4 w-4 ${isRiwayatActive ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-white/70"}`} />
              <span>Riwayat</span>
              <ChevronDown className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 ${isDropdownOpen ? "rotate-180" : ""}`} />
            </button>

            {isDropdownOpen && (
              <div className="absolute top-full left-0 mt-2 flex flex-col bg-white rounded-xl shadow-[0_15px_35px_rgba(0,0,0,.15)] p-2 min-w-[230px] z-[2000] border border-[#e0ece7] text-gray-800 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
                <Link
                  href="/riwayat/temuan"
                  onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(false)}
                  className={`rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[0.85rem] font-medium transition-colors no-underline flex items-center gap-2.5 ${
                    isActive("/riwayat/temuan")
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0d7565] to-[#125c50] text-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(13,117,101,0.25)] font-semibold"
                      : "text-[#1c3833] hover:bg-[#eef7f4] hover:text-[#0d7565]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <PackagePlus className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Taruh Barang</span>
                </Link>
                <Link
                  href="/riwayat/laporan"
                  onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(false)}
                  className={`rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[0.85rem] font-medium transition-colors no-underline flex items-center gap-2.5 ${
                    isActive("/riwayat/laporan")
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0d7565] to-[#125c50] text-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(13,117,101,0.25)] font-semibold"
                      : "text-[#1c3833] hover:bg-[#eef7f4] hover:text-[#0d7565]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Laporan Kehilangan</span>
                </Link>
                <Link
                  href="/riwayat/pengambilan"
                  onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen(false)}
                  className={`rounded-lg px-3.5 py-2.5 text-[0.85rem] font-medium transition-colors no-underline flex items-center gap-2.5 ${
                    isActive("/riwayat/pengambilan")
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0d7565] to-[#125c50] text-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(13,117,101,0.25)] font-semibold"
                      : "text-[#1c3833] hover:bg-[#eef7f4] hover:text-[#0d7565]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <PackageCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Pengambilan Barang</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\Notification.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  getNotifications,
  getUnreadCount,
  markAllAsRead,
  markOneAsRead,
  SerializedNotification,
} from "@/features/item/actions/notification";
import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/utils/dateFormat";
import { useClickOutside } from "@/hooks/useClickOutside";
import { useKeyboardShortcut } from "@/hooks/useKeyboardShortcut";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function Notification() {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<SerializedNotification[]>([]);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const fetchNotifs = async () => {
    try {
      const [count, notifs] = await Promise.all([
        getUnreadCount(),
        getNotifications(10),
      ]);
      setUnreadCount(count);
      setNotifications(notifs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Gagal load notifikasi:", err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let isSubscribed = true;

    const loadData = async () => {
      try {
        const [count, notifs] = await Promise.all([
          getUnreadCount(),
          getNotifications(10),
        ]);
        if (isSubscribed) {
          setUnreadCount(count);
          setNotifications(notifs);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Gagal load notifikasi:", err);
      }
    };

    void loadData();
    const interval = setInterval(loadData, 60000); // Polling cadangan 60s

    const supabase = createClient();
    const channel = supabase
      .channel("navbar-notifications-realtime")
      .on(
        "postgres_changes",
        { event: "*", schema: "public", table: "notifications" },
        () => {
          void loadData();
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => {
      isSubscribed = false;
      clearInterval(interval);
      supabase.removeChannel(channel);
    };
  }, []);

  // Close on Click Outside
  useClickOutside(dropdownRef, () => {
    setIsOpen(false);
  });

  // Close on Escape
  useKeyboardShortcut("Escape", () => {
    setIsOpen(false);
  });

  const handleToggle = () => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      fetchNotifs();
    }
    setIsOpen(!isOpen);
  };

  const handleMarkAll = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    await markAllAsRead();
    await fetchNotifs();
    setIsLoading(false);
  };

  const handleItemClick = async (notif: SerializedNotification) => {
    if (!notif.isRead) {
      await markOneAsRead(notif.id);
      fetchNotifs();
    }
    setIsOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
      {/* Tombol Lonceng */}
      <button
        onClick={handleToggle}
        className="relative p-2 text-white/80 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-full transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        aria-label="Notifikasi"
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        aria-haspopup="true"
      >
        <svg
          xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
          width="20"
          height="20"
          fill="currentColor"
          viewBox="0 0 16 16"
        >
          <path d="M8 16a2 2 0 0 0 2-2H6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2zM8 1.918l-.797.161A4.002 4.002 0 0 0 4 6c0 .628-.134 2.197-.459 3.742-.16.767-.376 1.566-.663 2.258h10.244c-.287-.692-.502-1.49-.663-2.258C12.134 8.197 12 6.628 12 6a4.002 4.002 0 0 0-3.203-3.92L8 1.917zM14.22 12c.223.447.481.801.78 1H1c.299-.199.557-.553.78-1C2.68 10.2 3 6.88 3 6c0-2.42 1.72-4.44 4.005-4.901a1 1 0 1 1 1.99 0A5.002 5.002 0 0 1 13 6c0 .88.32 4.2 1.22 6z" />
        </svg>

        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500 text-[10px] font-bold text-white shadow-sm">
            {unreadCount > 9 ? "9+" : unreadCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {/* Dropdown Notifikasi */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-80 sm:w-96 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 py-3 z-[2000] overflow-hidden text-gray-800 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 pb-2 border-b border-gray-100">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-green-dark flex items-center gap-2">
              Notifikasi
              {unreadCount > 0 && (
                <span className="bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold">
                  {unreadCount} baru
                </span>
              )}
            </h3>
            {unreadCount > 0 && (
              <button
                onClick={handleMarkAll}
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="text-xs text-green-accent hover:text-green-mid font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Tandai semua dibaca
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="max-h-80 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-gray-50">
            {notifications.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-gray-400">
                Belum ada notifikasi
              </div>
            ) : (
              notifications.map((notif) => (
                <div
                  key={notif.id}
                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(notif)}
                  className={`p-3.5 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${!notif.isRead ? "bg-emerald-50/50" : ""
                    }`}
                >
                  {notif.link ? (
                    <Link href={notif.link} className="block">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                        <p
                          className={`text-xs font-bold ${!notif.isRead ? "text-green-dark" : "text-gray-700"
                            }`}
                        >
                          {notif.title}
                        </p>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 shrink-0">
                          {timeAgo(notif.createdAt)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1 line-clamp-2">
                        {notif.message}
                      </p>
                    </Link>
                  ) : (
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                        <p
                          className={`text-xs font-bold ${!notif.isRead ? "text-green-dark" : "text-gray-700"
                            }`}
                        >
                          {notif.title}
                        </p>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 shrink-0">
                          {timeAgo(notif.createdAt)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1 line-clamp-2">
                        {notif.message}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: src\components\shared\RiwayatFAB.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useRef, ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Trash2, Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useClickOutside } from "@/hooks/useClickOutside";
import { useKeyboardShortcut } from "@/hooks/useKeyboardShortcut";

interface RiwayatFABProps {
  /** Href untuk navigasi Tambah Data */
  tambahHref: string;
  /** Label tooltip tombol Tambah Data */
  tambahLabel: string;
  /** Ikon tombol Tambah Data (ReactNode, mis. <PackagePlus className="h-5 w-5" />) */
  tambahIcon: ReactNode;
  /** Label tooltip tombol Hapus (mis. "Hapus Riwayat Temuan") */
  hapusLabel: string;
  /** Jumlah data aktif â€” tombol Hapus hanya tampil jika > 0 */
  jumlahData: number;
  /** Callback ketika tombol Hapus ditekan (setelah FAB ditutup) */
  onHapus: () => void;
}

/**
 * Expandable Floating Action Button (FAB) reusable untuk halaman riwayat.
 *
 * Desain dan perilaku identik dengan FAB Data Warga di WargaClient.tsx:
 * - Tombol utama: Plus (tertutup) -> X (terbuka), warna teal/abu-abu.
 * - Tombol Tambah Data: teal, navigasi via router.push().
 * - Tombol Hapus Data: merah, hanya tampil bila jumlahData > 0.
 * - Tutup via klik luar, Escape, atau klik tombol utama.
 * - Backdrop blur tipis di mobile saat FAB terbuka.
 */
export default function RiwayatFAB({
  tambahHref,
  tambahLabel,
  tambahIcon,
  hapusLabel,
  jumlahData,
  onHapus,
}: RiwayatFABProps) {
  const [isFabOpen, setIsFabOpen] = useState(false);
  const fabRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const router = useRouter();

  useClickOutside(fabRef, () => setIsFabOpen(false));
  useKeyboardShortcut("Escape", () => {
    if (isFabOpen) setIsFabOpen(false);
  });

  return (
    <>
      <div
        ref={fabRef}
        className="fixed bottom-8 right-8 z-[1000] flex flex-col-reverse items-end gap-3"
      >
        {/* Tombol Utama (Toggle) */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsFabOpen((prev) => !prev)}
          aria-expanded={isFabOpen}
          aria-label={isFabOpen ? "Tutup menu aksi" : "Buka menu aksi"}
          className={`flex items-center justify-center w-14 h-14 rounded-full shadow-xl transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer border-none outline-none ${
            isFabOpen
              ? "bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-800"
              : "bg-[#0d7565] hover:bg-[#0a5d50]"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="relative w-6 h-6">
            <Plus
              className={`h-6 w-6 text-white absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ${
                isFabOpen
                  ? "opacity-0 rotate-90 scale-75"
                  : "opacity-100 rotate-0 scale-100"
              }`}
            />
            <X
              className={`h-6 w-6 text-white absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ${
                isFabOpen
                  ? "opacity-100 rotate-0 scale-100"
                  : "opacity-0 -rotate-90 scale-75"
              }`}
            />
          </div>
        </button>

        {/* Tombol Tambah Data */}
        <div
          className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all duration-200 ${
            isFabOpen
              ? "opacity-100 translate-y-0 pointer-events-auto"
              : "opacity-0 translate-y-3 pointer-events-none"
          }`}
        >
          <span className="hidden sm:block bg-gray-900/85 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg shadow-lg whitespace-nowrap backdrop-blur-sm">
            {tambahLabel}
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setIsFabOpen(false);
              router.push(tambahHref);
            }}
            title={tambahLabel}
            className="flex items-center justify-center w-12 h-12 bg-[#0d7565] text-white rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-[#0a5d50] hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer border-none outline-none"
          >
            {tambahIcon}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tombol Hapus Data (hanya bila ada data) */}
        {jumlahData > 0 && (
          <div
            className={`flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all duration-200 ${
              isFabOpen
                ? "opacity-100 translate-y-0 pointer-events-auto"
                : "opacity-0 translate-y-3 pointer-events-none"
            }`}
            style={{ transitionDelay: isFabOpen ? "50ms" : "0ms" }}
          >
            <span className="hidden sm:block bg-gray-900/85 text-white text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg shadow-lg whitespace-nowrap backdrop-blur-sm">
              {hapusLabel} ({jumlahData})
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setIsFabOpen(false);
                onHapus();
              }}
              title={hapusLabel}
              className="flex items-center justify-center w-12 h-12 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-red-600 hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer border-none outline-none"
            >
              <Trash2 className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Backdrop mobile saat FAB terbuka */}
      {isFabOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[999] bg-black/20 backdrop-blur-[1px] sm:hidden"
          onClick={() => setIsFabOpen(false)}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\RouteChrome.tsx

"use client";

import { useUser } from "@clerk/nextjs";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import Navbar from "./Navbar";
import PublicNavbar from "../public/PublicNavbar";
import { isPetugasEmail } from "@/lib/petugas-access";
import { useChromeVisibility } from "./ChromeVisibility";

// Halaman tanpa navbar
const noNavbarPaths = new Set(["/access-denied"]);

// Halaman publik yang menampilkan PublicNavbar
const publicPaths = new Set(["/", "/pencarian", "/tentang-kami", "/beri-saran"]);

export default function RouteChrome({
  publicData,
}: {
  publicData: {
    categories: { id: string; name: string }[];
    colors: { id: string; name: string }[];
    brands: { id: string; name: string }[];
    locations: { id: string; name: string }[];
  };
}) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { isLoaded, isSignedIn, user } = useUser();
  const { isNotFound } = useChromeVisibility();

  if (isNotFound || pathname === "/test" || pathname.startsWith("/test/")) {
    return null;
  }

  if (noNavbarPaths.has(pathname)) {
    return null;
  }

  if (publicPaths.has(pathname)) {
    return <PublicNavbar {...publicData} />;
  }

  const isPetugas = user?.emailAddresses.some((email) =>
    isPetugasEmail(email.emailAddress)
  );

  if (!isLoaded || !isSignedIn || !isPetugas) {
    return null;
  }

  return <Navbar />;
}

"use client";

import { useUser } from "@clerk/nextjs";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { isPetugasEmail } from "@/lib/petugas-access";
import PublicFooter from "../public/PublicFooter";
import { useChromeVisibility } from "./ChromeVisibility";

const publicPaths = new Set(["/", "/pencarian", "/tentang-kami", "/beri-saran"]);
const noFooterPaths = new Set(["/access-denied"]);

export default function RouteFooter() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { isLoaded, isSignedIn, user } = useUser();
  const { isNotFound } = useChromeVisibility();
  const isPublicPage = publicPaths.has(pathname);

  if (
    isNotFound ||
    pathname === "/test" ||
    pathname.startsWith("/test/") ||
    noFooterPaths.has(pathname)
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  if (isPublicPage) {
    return <PublicFooter />;
  }

  const isPetugas = user?.emailAddresses.some((email) =>
    isPetugasEmail(email.emailAddress)
  );

  if (!isLoaded || !isSignedIn || !isPetugas) {
    return null;

  // Footer petugas hanya ditampilkan pada halaman dashboard
  if (pathname !== "/dashboard") {
    return null;

  return (
    <footer className="bg-[#0d3b2e] text-white/90 py-6 text-center text-xs border-t border-white/10">
      <div className="max-w-[1240px] mx-auto px-4">

        <p className="text-[11px] text-white/60 mt-1">Platform Terintegrasi Lost &amp; Found</p>
      </div>

  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\SearchBar.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter, usePathname, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";

export default function SearchBar() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();

  const urlQuery = searchParams.get("q") || "";
  const [query, setQuery] = useState(urlQuery);
  const [prevUrlQuery, setPrevUrlQuery] = useState(urlQuery);

  // Sync URL changes back to state (pola resmi React: adjusting state during render)
  if (urlQuery !== prevUrlQuery) {
    setPrevUrlQuery(urlQuery);
    setQuery(urlQuery);
  }

  const handleSearchChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    setQuery(e.target.value);
  };

  const handleSearchSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const currentQ = searchParams.get("q") || "";
    const cleanQuery = query.trim();

    if (cleanQuery !== currentQ.trim()) {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(searchParams.toString());
      if (cleanQuery) {
        params.set("q", cleanQuery);
      } else {
        params.delete("q");
      }
      router.replace(`${pathname}?${params.toString()}`);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSearchSubmit} className="w-full relative" role="search">
      <input
        type="text"
        value={query}
        onChange={handleSearchChange}
        placeholder="Cari data di halaman ini..."
        aria-label="Cari data di halaman ini"
        className="w-full bg-white/10 border border-white/20 rounded-full py-2.5 pl-5 pr-12 text-white placeholder:text-white/50 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:bg-white/20 focus:border-green-accent transition-all"
      />
      <button
        type="submit"
        className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white/60 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        aria-label="Cari"
        title="Cari"
      >
        <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 16 16">
          <path d="M11.742 10.344a6.5 6.5 0 1 0-1.397 1.398h-.001q.044.06.098.115l3.85 3.85a1 1 0 0 0 1.415-1.414l-3.85-3.85a1 1 0 0 0-.115-.1zM12 6.5a5.5 5.5 0 1 1-11 0 5.5 5.5 0 0 1 11 0"/>
        </svg>
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\shared\UserDatabaseSync.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useUser } from "@clerk/nextjs";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { syncUserToDatabase } from "@/lib/sync-user";

export default function UserDatabaseSync() {
  const { isLoaded, isSignedIn, user } = useUser();
  const syncedUserId = useRef<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoaded) return;

    if (!isSignedIn || !user) {
      syncedUserId.current = null;
      return;
    }

    if (syncedUserId.current === user.id) return;

    syncedUserId.current = user.id;
    void syncUserToDatabase().catch((error: unknown) => {
      syncedUserId.current = null;
      console.error("Gagal melakukan sinkronisasi user:", error);
    });
  }, [isLoaded, isSignedIn, user]);

  return null;
}

FILE: src\components\ui\Badge.tsx

import React from "react";

export interface BadgeProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  variant?: "brand" | "primary" | "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "info" | "neutral";
  className?: string;
}

export function Badge({ children, variant = "neutral", className = "" }: BadgeProps) {
  let colorStyles = "";
  switch (variant) {
    case "brand":
      colorStyles = "bg-[#0d3b2e] text-white border border-[#0d3b2e]";
      break;
    case "primary":
      colorStyles = "bg-[#eef7f4] text-[#0d7565] border border-[#d2e5df]";
      break;
    case "success":
      colorStyles = "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200";
      break;
    case "warning":
      colorStyles = "bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200";
      break;
    case "danger":
      colorStyles = "bg-red-50 text-red-800 border border-red-200";
      break;
    case "info":
      colorStyles = "bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 border border-sky-200";
      break;
    case "neutral":
      colorStyles = "bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200";
      break;
  }

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide ${colorStyles} ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\ui\Button.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: "primary" | "brand" | "secondary" | "danger" | "outline" | "ghost";
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
}

export function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  size = "md",
  className = "",
  children,
  ...props
}: ButtonProps) {
  const baseStyles =
    "inline-flex items-center justify-center font-semibold rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed select-none gap-2";

  let sizeStyles = "h-11 px-4.5 text-xs sm:text-sm";
  if (size === "sm") {
    sizeStyles = "h-9 px-3 text-xs";
  } else if (size === "lg") {
    sizeStyles = "h-12 px-6 text-sm font-bold";
  }

  let variantStyles = "";
  switch (variant) {
    case "primary":
      variantStyles =
        "bg-[#0d7565] hover:bg-[#0a5d50] active:scale-[0.98] text-white shadow-xs hover:shadow-md";
      break;
    case "brand":
      variantStyles =
        "bg-[#0d3b2e] hover:bg-[#1a5c44] active:scale-[0.98] text-white shadow-xs hover:shadow-md";
      break;
    case "secondary":
      variantStyles =
        "bg-[#eef7f4] text-[#0d7565] hover:bg-[#e0f1eb] active:scale-[0.98] border border-[#d2e5df]";
      break;
    case "danger":
      variantStyles =
        "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:scale-[0.98] text-white shadow-xs";
      break;
    case "outline":
      variantStyles =
        "border border-[#d2e5df] text-[#142e29] bg-white hover:bg-[#f6faf8] active:scale-[0.98]";
      break;
    case "ghost":
      variantStyles =
        "bg-transparent text-[#526b65] hover:bg-[#f0f5f3] hover:text-[#102824] shadow-none";
      break;
  }

  return (
    <button className={`${baseStyles} ${sizeStyles} ${variantStyles} ${className}`} {...props}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\ui\CategoryIcon.tsx
================================================================================
import React from "react";
import {
  Headphones,
  Laptop,
  Smartphone,
  Wallet,
  FileText,
  ShoppingBag,
  Backpack,
  Shirt,
  CloudRain,
  Activity,
  Footprints,
  Watch,
  CupSoda,
  Utensils,
  Glasses,
  Home,
  Key,
  Sparkles,
  BookOpen,
  Package,
  Tag,
  LucideProps,
} from "lucide-react";

interface CategoryIconProps extends LucideProps {
  categoryName: string;
  size?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export function CategoryIcon({
  categoryName,
  size = 20,
  className = "",
  ...props
}: CategoryIconProps) {
  const nameLower = (categoryName || "").toLowerCase().trim();

  let IconComponent = Tag;

  if (nameLower.includes("elektronik")) {
    IconComponent = Headphones;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("komputer") || nameLower.includes("laptop")) {
    IconComponent = Laptop;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("handphone") || nameLower.includes("hp") || nameLower.includes("ponsel")) {
    IconComponent = Smartphone;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("dompet") || nameLower.includes("uang")) {
    IconComponent = Wallet;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("dokumen") || nameLower.includes("kertas") || nameLower.includes("surat")) {
    IconComponent = FileText;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("tas") || nameLower.includes("ransel")) {
    IconComponent = Backpack;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("jaket") || nameLower.includes("kemeja") || nameLower.includes("kaos") || nameLower.includes("pakaian")) {
    IconComponent = Shirt;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("hujan") || nameLower.includes("jas hujan")) {
    IconComponent = CloudRain;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("olahraga")) {
    IconComponent = Activity;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("sepatu") || nameLower.includes("sandal")) {
    IconComponent = Footprints;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("jam")) {
    IconComponent = Watch;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("botol") || nameLower.includes("tumbler") || nameLower.includes("minum")) {
    IconComponent = CupSoda;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("makan") || nameLower.includes("alat makan")) {
    IconComponent = Utensils;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("kacamata")) {
    IconComponent = Glasses;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("rumah")) {
    IconComponent = Home;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("kunci")) {
    IconComponent = Key;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("aksesoris") || nameLower.includes("perhiasan")) {
    IconComponent = Sparkles;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("buku") || nameLower.includes("tulis")) {
    IconComponent = BookOpen;
  } else if (nameLower.includes("lain")) {
    IconComponent = Package;
  }

  return <IconComponent size={size} className={className} strokeWidth={2} {...props} />;
}

export default CategoryIcon;

FILE: src\components\ui\Input.tsx

import React, { useId } from "react";

export interface InputProps extends React.InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
}

export const Input = React.forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputProps>(
  ({ label, error, className = "", id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const generatedId = useId();
    const inputId = id || (label ? `input-${generatedId}` : undefined);

    return (
      <div className="w-full">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={inputId} className="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1.5 text-[#142e29]">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <input
          ref={ref}
          id={inputId}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          className={`w-full border border-[#d2e5df] rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#142e29] placeholder:text-[#8ba39c] bg-white transition-all outline-none focus:border-[#0d7565] focus:ring-3 focus:ring-[#0d7565]/15 ${
            error ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/15" : ""
          } ${className}`}
          {...props}
        />
        {error && <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-red-600 mt-1">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Input.displayName = "Input";

FILE: src\components\ui\ItemCard.tsx

import React from "react";
import NextImage from "next/image";
import { MapPin, Calendar, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import {
  PublicFoundItem,
  getItemTitle,
  getCategoryRepresentativeImage,
  formatIndonesianDate,
  DEFAULT_ITEM_IMAGE,
} from "@/features/item/utils/public-item-utils";

interface ItemCardProps {
  item: PublicFoundItem;
  onClick?: () => void;
  className?: string;
}

export function ItemCard({ item, onClick, className = "" }: ItemCardProps) {
  const title = getItemTitle(item);
  const imageUrl = getCategoryRepresentativeImage(item.jenis.name);
  const dateStr = formatIndonesianDate(item.createdAt);

  const handleMouseEnter = () => {
    // Preload item image for instant modal rendering
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && imageUrl) {
      const img = new window.Image();
      img.src = imageUrl;
    }
  };

  return (
    <article
      onClick={onClick}
      onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
      onTouchStart={handleMouseEnter}
      className={`group flex cursor-pointer flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#e2ece8] bg-white text-left shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.03)] transition-all duration-250 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#158a76]/60 hover:shadow-[0_12px_24px_rgba(13,59,46,0.12)] active:scale-[0.99] ${className}`}
    >
      {/* Image Banner */}
      <div className="relative h-[155px] w-full overflow-hidden bg-[#eef4f1]">
        <NextImage
          src={imageUrl || DEFAULT_ITEM_IMAGE}
          alt={title}
          fill

}

FILE: src\components\ui\Modal.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useKeyboardShortcut } from "@/hooks/useKeyboardShortcut";

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Modal({ isOpen, onClose, title, children, className = "" }: ModalProps) {
  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Close on Escape
  useKeyboardShortcut("Escape", () => {
    if (isOpen) onClose();
  });

  // Focus trap
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const modalElement = modalRef.current;
    if (!modalElement) return;

    const focusableElements = modalElement.querySelectorAll(
      'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
    );
    const firstElement = focusableElements[0] as HTMLElement;
    const lastElement = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1] as HTMLElement;

    const handleTabKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key !== "Tab") return;

      if (e.shiftKey) {
        if (document.activeElement === firstElement) {
          lastElement.focus();
          e.preventDefault();
        }
      } else {
        if (document.activeElement === lastElement) {
          firstElement.focus();
          e.preventDefault();
        }
      }
    };

    modalElement.addEventListener("keydown", handleTabKey);
    // Focus first focusable element or modal container
    if (firstElement) {
      firstElement.focus();
    } else {
      modalElement.focus();
    }

    // Add scroll lock
    document.body.classList.add("modal-open");

    return () => {
      modalElement.removeEventListener("keydown", handleTabKey);
      document.body.classList.remove("modal-open");
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const hasCustomMaxWidth = className.includes("max-w-");

  return (
    <div 
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[2000] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 md:p-6 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={modalRef}
        tabIndex={-1}
        className={`bg-white w-full ${hasCustomMaxWidth ? "" : "max-w-md"} rounded-2xl shadow-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 relative outline-none modal-animate-in ${className}`}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
      >
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20 w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          aria-label="Tutup"
        >
          âœ•
        </button>
        <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold mb-4 text-green-dark shrink-0 pr-8">
          {title}
        </h2>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\components\ui\PrefetchLink.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import React, { useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import Link, { LinkProps } from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

interface PrefetchLinkProps extends LinkProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  onPrefetchData?: () => void;
  target?: string;
  rel?: string;
  onClick?: (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => void;
}

export function PrefetchLink({
  children,
  href,
  className = "",
  onPrefetchData,
  onClick,
  ...props
}: PrefetchLinkProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const prefetchedRef = useRef(false);

  const handlePrefetch = useCallback(() => {
    if (prefetchedRef.current) return;
    prefetchedRef.current = true;

    // Prefetch Next.js route JS & RSC payload
    if (typeof href === "string") {
      router.prefetch(href);
    }

    // Call custom data prefetch handler (e.g. API payload caching)
    if (onPrefetchData) {
      onPrefetchData();
    }
  }, [href, router, onPrefetchData]);

  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className={className}
      onMouseEnter={handlePrefetch}
      onTouchStart={handlePrefetch}
      onFocus={handlePrefetch}
      onClick={onClick}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
}

import React, { useId } from "react";

export interface SelectProps extends React.SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  options: { value: string; label: string }[];
  placeholder?: string;
}

export const Select = React.forwardRef<HTMLSelectElement, SelectProps>(
  ({ label, error, options, placeholder, className = "", id, ...props }, ref) => {
    const generatedId = useId();
    const selectId = id || (label ? `select-${generatedId}` : undefined);

    return (
      <div className="w-full">
        {label && (
          <label htmlFor={selectId} className="block text-xs font-semibold mb-1.5 text-[#142e29]">
            {label}
          </label>
        )}
        <select
          ref={ref}
          id={selectId}
          aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
          className={`w-full border border-[#d2e5df] rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#142e29] bg-white transition-all outline-none focus:border-[#0d7565] focus:ring-3 focus:ring-[#0d7565]/15 cursor-pointer ${
            error ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500/15" : ""
          } ${className}`}
          defaultValue=""
          {...props}
        >
          {placeholder && (
            <option value="" disabled hidden>
              {placeholder}
            </option>
          )}
          {options.map((opt) => (
            <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>
              {opt.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        {error && <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-red-600 mt-1">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
Select.displayName = "Select";

import React from "react";

interface TableProps {

  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Table({ headers, children, className = "" }: TableProps) {
  return (
    <div className={`bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-x-auto ${className}`}>
      <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse min-w-[700px]">
        <thead>
          <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-100 text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {headers.map((h, i) => {
              const isAction = h.toLowerCase() === "aksi";
              return (
                <th
                  key={i}
                  className={`px-6 py-4 ${isAction ? "text-center w-24" : "text-left"}`}
                >
                  {h}
                </th>
              );
            })}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
          {children}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src\features\claim\actions.ts
